# Scenario One shared-save G0: current source and fixture provenance audit

**Task:** [#537](https://github.com/jermur788/CCF/issues/537), research child of [#535](https://github.com/jermur788/CCF/issues/535). **Date:** 2026-11-15. **Worker:** OpenAI GPT-6.1 Sol (`gpt-6.1-sol`), reasoning effort **high**, through T3 Code/Codex. **State:** evidence handoff for manager review; no implementation or product-policy approval.

**Branch:** `docs/scen1-g0-save-source-audit-20261115-v1`. **Worktree:** `/media/jer/Files/CCF/Operations/CCF-537-G0-SaveAudit`. **Source/base:** freshly fetched `origin/main`, full HEAD **`fc18190b976f1363f1b5d1b0881810db2f8c6e39`**. The requested branch/path did not exist; a new worktree was created from the bare store. Completed checkout had clean status before research/writing. Context-source commit, calculated from canonical sources/instructions/generator: `62e39ec621e38a84030c08b35a171e297056c9ed`. Coordination read from fetched main, not treated as part of that context snapshot. User-reported dirty anchor files were left alone.

**Scope:** exactly this new tracked Markdown file. No source, spec, package, serialized asset, canonical project record, real save or Reference file changed. No Unity lease/state access, Editor, Player, compiler, test harness or actual save/load execution. GitHub/document reading and source inspection are **not compile or runtime evidence**. Python JSON/gzip/hash inspection below is byte-level evidence only.

**Highest-risk source finding:** current load admission does not authenticate a world/profile: a foreign scenario ID merely warns after restoring the forest (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1481`), and saving still targets one shared filename (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:15`). The fallback overwrite is non-atomic (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:85`), and there is no backup recovery loader. Current behavior and genuine fixture gaps must be reconciled before the draft router becomes implementation authority.

## 2. Serialization, raw version and disk-write semantics

### Actual schema and raw/typed distinction

`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:7` declares `CurrentVersion = 24`; line 9 declares **`public int version = CurrentVersion;`**. `Save` builds a fresh DTO (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:109`) and pretty-serializes it using `JsonUtility.ToJson(data, true)` (line 44). Disk reads use `JsonUtility.FromJson<ForestSaveData>(json)` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:268`). There is no envelope, profile ID or root world/content identity in this DTO (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:5`, fields through line 49).

| Input | Source-derived behavior; runtime limit |
| --- | --- |
| Raw `version` absent | The declared initializer is 24; the validation source explicitly describes an empty JSON result as having “current version” (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.cs:6`). No raw version-presence check exists in `TryReadSave` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:267`). Therefore absence must **not** be modelled as automatically parsed numeric zero. Exact Unity `FromJson` missing-field behavior is **NOT VERIFIED by execution in this audit**. |
| Explicit numeric 0 / negative | If parsed as that number, `LoadData` normalizes it to 1 at `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:327`; `CanLoad` does the same at line 249. Disk JSON checks execute **before** that normalization (lines 268–274), using the original parsed version. This is not equivalent to an absent field taking the declared current initializer. |
| Explicit newer numeric version | All applicable raw/semantic checks still apply; if successful, `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:346` logs “newer than supported … loading best-effort” and proceeds. `CanLoad` has no separate future-version refusal (lines 249–257). |

For current-class raw-absent version, a legacy-shaped file may consequently face current-schema presence checks: geometry at `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.Geometry.cs:27`, storm model at `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.Storms.cs:61`, rack state at `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.RackV2.cs:165`, objective history at `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.cs:438`, prescriptions at line 633. This is a **source-derived compatibility risk**, not a newly reproduced serializer result. The comment “Saves written before versioning load as version 1” (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:326`) is broader than its actual `<= 0` condition proves.

### Files, order and failure windows

The one current slot is `Path.Combine(Application.persistentDataPath, "forest-save.json")`; `.tmp` and `.bak` suffixes are fixed (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:9`, lines 15–19). The menu separately duplicates the same filename (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:77`). Runtime absolute persistentDataPath was **NOT VERIFIED**; known host files are byte-inspected in §8.

1. Open `.tmp` with `FileMode.Create`, exclusive write, UTF-8 without BOM; write, flush writer, then `stream.Flush(true)` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:64`–69).
2. If main absent, `File.Move(temp, SavePath)`; this branch does not create a new backup (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:72`–75). Existing backup handling is not otherwise validated here.
3. If main exists, `File.Replace(temp, SavePath, BackupSavePath)` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:79`). The old file is preserved as backup by the API, **without checking that it was a good/compatible save**. Comments saying “previous good save” (line 18) express intent, not validation.
4. Only `PlatformNotSupportedException` selects fallback: `File.Copy(main, bak, true)`, `File.Copy(temp, main, true)`, `File.Delete(temp)` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:81`–86). This is a **non-atomic overwrite**; interruption/failure during the second copy can leave main partial, while backup may retain previous bytes. There is no rollback code. Actual platform/API failure outcomes are **NOT VERIFIED**.
5. `finally` deletes surviving temp, swallowing only `IOException` from deletion (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:89`–95). A process crash does not guarantee `finally`; a stale temp can remain and next save truncates it. The loader ignores temp. Directory fsync, post-write reread and checksum verification are not implemented in this function (lines 59–98).
6. `Save` catches I/O/access errors and says **“Your previous save is unchanged”** (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:46`–49). The fallback overwrite window means that unconditional message is not established by source. Capture occurs before the `try` (line 41); unrelated capture/serialization exceptions and arbitrary restoration exceptions have no transactional rollback here.

Backup is only mentioned when a read/parse exception is caught (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:281`–288). Missing main returns early (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:223`); semantic/raw-presence refusals do not attempt backup. The code has no user-facing backup recovery API to extend unchanged: B-09 requires new approved engineering/UX work.

## 3. Validation order and mutations before admission (B-10)

**Disk load:** file-existence check → `ReadAllText` → `JsonUtility.FromJson` → raw validators in short-circuit order **geometry, storm, competition, Rack V2, objective history, prescriptions** (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:223`, lines 267–274) → `LoadData` → normalize parsed version → gather scene objects → `FindLoadProblem` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:327`–337). No disk writes occur on this load chain.

**Semantic preflight:** derive save geometry → validate DTO → model-2 grid/patch applicability → spawner availability → authoritative species resolution (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:301`–318). DTO validation checks null/tree list, geometry, scenario cell indices, tree count/IDs/states, storms/models/cohorts, v15 state, racks, objective history, management event IDs, prescriptions, in that order (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.cs:24`–149). Species resolver reads configured species and returns null for unknown (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestTreeSpawner.cs:95`–107).

**First load mutation after these checks:** `ecology.StandApproachExpected = ...`, geometry/grid/approach reconciliation (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:355`–357). Then carried wood (line 361), deactivate/destroy extra trees (line 384), restore/spawn trees (lines 403, 422), marks/buildables/storage (lines 458, 471, 483), ecology/cohorts/canopy/seed (lines 496–510), finally management restore (line 517). There is no commit/rollback transaction around this sequence.

The useful guarantee is narrow: implemented semantic/geometry/species refusals occur before the first scene mutation **inside `LoadData`**. It is **not** a profile/identity admission guarantee:

| Route | State changed before relevant validation |
| --- | --- |
| Foreign nested `scenarioId` on ordinary load | There is **no refusing identity validator**. Warning only at `Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1481`, after controller geometry/tree/ecology changes and manager `TeachingWorldVersion++`, quote invalidation, rack clearing and browse-protection replacement (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1456`–1471). Restore continues at line 1483. |
| Direct `LoadData(dto)` | It never receives raw JSON or runs the six raw-presence validators (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:324`–337 versus line 269). It also mutates caller-owned `data.version` to 1 before validation (line 328). This is DTO mutation, separate from live-world mutation. |
| Direct `RestoreSaveData` | Public overload supplies current version (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1451`) and the versioned overload immediately mutates live management state at line 1456. It bypasses controller preflight entirely. One tracked verification invokes it directly (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOneInteractionVerification.cs:289`); Appendix A includes other callers. |
| Preview enter | Identity/frozen hash checked first (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:677`, 689), but capture can initialize live model-2 understorey, and panel/year/preview flags change **before** typed-load validation (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:694`, lines 704–707). On typed-load refusal flags are reset, but the Work Plan is not explicitly reopened (lines 709–715). |
| Preview return | Clears `referencePreviewActive` before load (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:725`). On refusal it logs error then discards `previewReturnData`, restores pose and says “Returned” (lines 727–740); this is not an all-or-nothing rollback. Missing controller/data also skips forest restore. |
| Capture/save | `CaptureData` calls `CaptureSaveData` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:134`); model 2 calls `EnsureUnderstoreyGrid` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1398`), which can clear/repopulate live understorey and invalidate competition (lines 2525–2533). Thus capture is not universally pure. Public `Save()` has no preview/profile/DTO validation guard (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:39`–44); only input/menu opening guard preview (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:30`, `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:80`). |
| Startup/new/restart | `ForestStartingStand.Awake` applies new-game geometry/generates trees (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestStartingStand.cs:68`–70); manager Awake applies models/geometry and initializes management (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:472`–486). Menu bootstrap runs **AfterSceneLoad** (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:28`) and Continue later validates. Current startup therefore creates a fresh live world before selection; a future profile selector cannot promise pre-creation validation by wrapping only `Load`. |
| Continue availability vs execution | `CanLoad` itself performs read/semantic checks without scene writes (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:240`–257), but successful Continue dismisses help/closes menu before `Load` rereads disk (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.Startup.cs:102`–105). No same-bytes token binds the two reads; concurrent file change remains a risk. |

## 4. Historical admission: implementation versus intent

| Case | What the code decides |
| --- | --- |
| Pre-v20 geometry absent or explicit numeric 0 | `Assets/ForestPrototype/StandGeometryModel.cs:60` selects Legacy40; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.Geometry.cs:14` accepts only typed zero on pre-v20. Thus explicit 0 is permitted; nonzero is rejected even though geometry dispatch otherwise selects legacy. Raw field-presence validation is skipped below v20 (geometry validator line 27). |
| Pre-v20 gameplay layers | No blanket minimum-version rejection. Versions <3 do not restore ecology; <7 use default species; <13 marks use historical list; regeneration <16/growth <17/storm <19 restore legacy/off (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:394`, lines 443–461, 490–505). All applicable validators can still refuse malformed records; “every legacy version still passes” is a comment (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.cs:10`), not evidence that every historical file passes. |
| v1–v9 management | Controller passes null scenario (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:517`); manager initializes configured opening management while forest/ecology continues (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1473`–1479). This is current behavior, not save migration on disk. |
| v10–v24 absent nested scenario ID / null scenario | No controller or semantic-validator identity rejection. Nonempty foreign IDs only warn (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1481`); empty/null ID skips warning. Null scenario initializes management (line 1473). Declared `ScenarioOneSaveData.scenarioId` defaults to `"scenario-one"` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:57`), so raw absence and explicit empty are not distinguishable reliably after typed parsing alone. Exact absent-object/initializer behavior remains **NOT VERIFIED in Unity**. Genuine missing-ID v10–v24 fixture coverage is absent from the established provenance set. |
| Unknown/foreign scenario ID | Subject to other validators, accepted with late warning, not rejected (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1481`–1483). This ID is a scenario/chapter string, not a world/profile discriminator. Tightening it is a compatibility/product decision; this audit does not choose it. |
| Unknown species IDs | Nonempty authoritative tree/cohort/juvenile/open-order species are rejected; empty keeps historical default, resolved-order/history text not checked (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.cs:348`–373). Do not confuse species ID protection with scenario/profile protection. |
| Bad tree/event IDs | Missing/duplicate tree IDs reject (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.cs:50`–53); present management events must have unique positive IDs across all versions (lines 155–167). “Missing nested ID” must name the field; it cannot mean all IDs are tolerated. |
| Newer-than-v24 | Warn + best effort after validation, not unconditional acceptance (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:346`). No migration engine for unknown future fields exists; capture always constructs current v24 DTO (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:7`–9; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:109`). Therefore loading a compatible future payload then saving can drop unknown data. Actual future-file round trip **NOT VERIFIED**. |

Later fields have explicit in-memory old-version normalization: pre-v15 shelter/execution fields, pre-v21 empty legacy rack plan, pre-v22 empty objective history, pre-v23 empty prescriptions (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneSaveMigration.cs:7`, lines 29, 44, 53). The v24 first-review latch restores true only for v24+ (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1484`). None of this proves a complete authentic version corpus exists.

## 5. Geometry declaration, validation and dispatch (B-11)

Current supported numeric models are exactly **Legacy40=0**, **Enlarged80=1** (`Assets/ForestPrototype/StandGeometryModel.cs:15`–17); `IsKnown` explicitly lists both (line 32). Size is **`model == Enlarged80 ? 80f : 40f`** (line 34), with 5m cells (line 19) and derived 64/256-cell grids (lines 36–41). Fresh production Scenario One selects Enlarged80 (policy line 72); verification override exists only under `UNITY_EDITOR` (lines 74–110).

`standGeometryModel` has an implicit numeric-zero default (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:32`). Capture writes live geometry (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:122`); disk v20+ requires explicit integer token matching parsed value (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.Geometry.cs:31`–34). Typed `LoadData` cannot establish raw field presence. Unknown typed geometry is refused by `ValidateGeometry` (line 18), and public `ApplyStandGeometry` independently throws for unknown values (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestEcologyController.cs:94`).

**Does a new geometry silently fall through?** Today an unknown value cannot pass the ordinary save-load/apply guards. However, the **size helper directly falls through to 40m**, and starting-stand generation branches only on `== Enlarged80`, then runs legacy generation (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestStartingStand.cs:83`–89). Merely adding a third value to `IsKnown` would expose silent legacy behavior unless these dispatch sites change too. The audit found 99 textual Legacy40/Enlarged80 references in `Assets/ForestPrototype/*.cs` recursively; not all are dispatchers, but a compact extension needs a bounded caller review, not one new constant.

Other divergent consumers include: geometry applies grid/approach/event (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestEcologyController.cs:98`–107); boundary subscribes to that event (`Assets/ForestPrototype/StandWorldBoundary.cs:47`); approach is Enlarged80-only (`Assets/ForestPrototype/StandApproachPath.cs:39`); V2 racks require Enlarged80 (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveValidation.RackV2.cs:35`); suggested racks require Enlarged80 (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneSuggestedRackLayout.cs:32`); objectives consume geometry for effective targets (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneObjectives.cs:52`–62); Reference is permanently Legacy40 (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioReferenceArchive.cs:110`). No new enum value, compact geometry or ID is selected here.

## 6. Frozen Reference Future v1 and preview identity (B-07)

The resource name is `ScenarioOneReferenceFutureV1` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioReferenceArchive.cs:24`); actual tracked bytes are `Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/Resources/ScenarioOneReferenceFutureV1.bytes`. `Load` decompresses GZip, parses archive via `JsonUtility`, and hashes each original embedded world after removing whitespace outside quoted strings; it never reserializes the historical DTO to establish integrity (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioReferenceArchive.cs:45`–95). `Hash` is UTF-8 FNV-1a, unsigned 64-bit (lines 185–193). Contract requires frozen archive/preview/current-ecology continuation, not exact historical biology replay (`Docs/ReferenceFutureContract.md:21`–35).

`IdentityMatches` requires scenario ID match, exact reference ID, archive definition `scenario-one-v12`, live definition v12 or v13, archive saveVersion 12 within current support, equal simulation seed, and verified year 0/100 (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioReferenceArchive.cs:121`–128). `Matches` additionally requires live Legacy40 (line 116). **Preview deliberately uses `IdentityMatches`, not `Matches`** (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:677`) so current Enlarged80 can visit Legacy40 and return. Current authored asset has `scenarioId: scenario-one`, `definitionVersion: scenario-one-v13` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOne.asset:15`–16).

This confirms the #527/#533 chapter-level observation: there is **no world/profile identity parameter** in that match. A future compact profile retaining this chapter/definition/seed could satisfy it. Restricting preview to historical O1 therefore requires an approved active-profile capability check at public entry and UI, not changing the frozen archive or relying on geometry mismatch (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:675`–677 intentionally exclude it). This is a design consequence, not a decision to disable anything now.

Preview copies frozen milestone through current DTO in memory, verifies `verifiedFrozenWorld`, captures return world, stores player position/rotation/pitch and camera rotation, sets preview active, then typed-loads (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:688`–717). Return reloads memory snapshot and separate pose (lines 721–740). F5/F9 are blocked by controller input guard; F10 cannot open during preview (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:80`). **Public `Save`, `Load`, `LoadData` are not intrinsically preview-safe**; §3 documents failure/mutation limits. No preview disk writes were executed in this audit.

## 7. Preferences: exact current keys and sharing

No PlayerPrefs or EditorPrefs is used by `ForestSaveController` itself (entire file, `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:7`–end); save selection comes solely from persistentDataPath plus filename (line 15).

| Store/key | Deciding source | Sharing/reset scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `PlayerPrefs`: `CCF.Learning.v1.` + step ID | `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/LearningObjectivesView.Curriculum.cs:9`; reads/writes `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/LearningObjectivesView.cs:98`, line 107 | No world/profile ID in key. `ResetProgress` deletes all curriculum keys and saves (lines 87–90). |
| `PlayerPrefs`: `CCF.MenuHelp.v1.` + enum name | `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/MenuHelpView.cs:11`, line 64, line 154 | Exact suffixes `WalkingHud`, `TreeInspection`, `StandMap`, `WorkPlan`, `AnnualReview`, `Rack`, `TreeMarks` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/MenuHelpView.Text.cs:7`). Reset deletes every enum entry (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/MenuHelpView.cs:68`–71). |
| `EditorPrefs`: `ScenarioOneInteractionVerification.Requested` | `Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOneInteractionVerification.cs:15`, line 19 | Editor verification launch flag, not player/save progress |
| `EditorPrefs`: `ScenarioHabitatPresentationVerification.Requested` | `Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioHabitatPresentationVerification.cs:12`, line 57 | Editor verification launch flag |
| `EditorPrefs`: `CCF.RecentAssetReviewVerification` | `Assets/ForestPrototype/RecentAssetReviewVerification.cs:11`, line 15 | Editor verification launch flag |

Exact learning suffixes and declaration lines are listed in Appendix B. Current keys are shared between forests **within the same application's preference store**; they do not include a profile discriminator. Cross-application/OS persistence locations and actual current values were **NOT VERIFIED**. Session reset calls UI teaching reset (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:136`). Durable world teaching fact `firstGuidedRackReviewComplete` is separate save data (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:56`); preferences cannot be substituted for it.

## 8. Fixture inventory and provenance

Appendix C records every save-shaped artifact found by the stated bounded scans with exact path, byte count, SHA-256, raw version/geometry, identity and provenance classification. **A save-shaped JSON file is not automatically a genuine user save.** Files with unresolved origin are explicitly `NOT VERIFIED`; runtime harness outputs are distinct from synthetic mutations and user archives.

Searches: all tracked JSON in this checkout, tracked Reference resource/schedule, durable `/media/jer/Files/CCF/` non-cache evidence/archive/build directories, explicit T-05/T-08 fixture directories, and known host Unity save paths. Registered worktrees/source assets/caches were pruned from the broad durable scan; tracked source fixtures were scanned separately. Other people's orchestration worktree and `/media/jer/ZX20/CCF-Ops/state/unity/` were excluded. Archives inside zip/tar/bundle containers, active ZX20 lanes, every alternate user/OS save root and unlinked machines were **NOT VERIFIED**. Historical `/media/jer/ZX20/CCF-Verification/` maps to Files verification per read-only dirty anchor `/media/jer/Files/CCF-Primary/Docs/Operations/StorageArchitecture.md:75`; that file is absent from this pinned main snapshot, so the current physical paths are recorded directly rather than silently rewriting historical citations.

Established provenance anchors:

Schedule size **9254**, SHA-256 **`91cf50595e9b3337438323ef09609a70d7de11374569dd9fb3c6359fbdb798f0`**. Raw embedded years 0/20/50/100 each explicitly version 12, geometry field absent (Legacy40 under source rules), nested scenario ID `scenario-one`. Archive definition v12 and simulation seed 20260914 match its metadata. Stored milestone hashes: `A564039D9B7CE31D`, `F7DF7DAB53B6FD32`, `D5E75D6D21D631AC`, `7AD177B3CC2F73C7`; stored schedule `56C8B99FA1E8DDD1` (`Docs/ReferenceFutureContract.md:9`–13). **A genuine v10–v24 corpus is absent from the provenance-established set.** Found version numbers alone do not fill provenance gaps. In particular, authentic missing-ID/raw-absent-version v10–v24 coverage and a complete per-version manifest were not located; the known genuine v20/v21/v22 samples all have explicit scenario-one IDs. Broad candidates at other versions need origin review before use. This is a release risk, **not permission to fabricate, downgrade headers or claim compatibility passed**.

## 9. Minimum implementable route — conditional design sketch only

This is a technical seam proposal with risks, **not implementation, approved policy, registry literals or a product choice**. It depends on formal spec approval and source ownership.

1. Put a small compile-time profile record/allowlist at the current filename boundary and pass an explicit selected record through both controller and menu, rather than retaining two independent path literals (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:15`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:77`). Freeze legacy slot bytes/keys/admission behavior unless authority explicitly changes them. Exact new names/envelope/counters remain B-01/B-02/B-13 decisions.
2. Separate **read and validate** from **apply** around existing `TryReadSave`/`FindLoadProblem` (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:261`, line 296). Parse raw identity/version before DTO defaults can erase presence, validate new profiles against their selected profile record, retain legacy compatibility rules separately. Bind eventual apply to the exact admitted bytes/DTO/profile so `CanLoad` is not a write or an authentication token. Do not call `CaptureData` before required no-mutation admission; it can initialize understorey (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1398`).
3. Route public Save/Load/CanLoad/LoadData and relevant direct scenario restoration through one explicit policy boundary. Restrict any trusted in-memory archive/return seam to a narrow authenticated origin plus unchanged semantic validation, not a public caller boolean bypass (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:324`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:707`, 727, 1454). Guard preview at public save/load entry, not only F5/F9/F10 UI (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:30` versus line 39). Preservation of existing genuine legacy admissions is a separate policy gate, not a side effect of strict new-profile validation.
4. Decide intended active profile before world generation/scene transition. Existing `AfterSceneLoad` menu and Awake generation are too late for a no-scene-creation promise (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:28`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestStartingStand.cs:68`). A minimal early selection/bootstrap seam or staged destination world is needed; the exact lifecycle/rollback strategy must be qualified, not promised from this source audit.
5. Treat geometry and profile/world identity separately. Implement explicit three-way geometry dispatch and unknown rejection at helpers/generator/consumers before enabling compact, preserving historical numeric meanings (`Assets/ForestPrototype/StandGeometryModel.cs:32`–34; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestStartingStand.cs:83`). Keep O2 unavailable until its real authored identity/bootstrap is supplied; species assets alone do not supply that world.
6. Derive `.tmp`/`.bak` from the admitted slot; design same-slot opt-in recovery through the same preflight. Current fallback copy needs honest partial-write handling and qualified failure cases, not reuse of the unconditional “unchanged” claim (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:84`–85, 49). No recovery filename/UI rule is chosen here.
7. Preserve historical learning/menu prefixes; new profiles need explicit approved namespaces and reset scope. Keep historical Reference bytes and add an active-profile capability check if approved (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/LearningObjectivesView.Curriculum.cs:9`, `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/MenuHelpView.cs:11`, `Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioReferenceArchive.cs:121`). Inventory verification callers before changing API shape (Appendix A).

Future isolated verification should cover genuine original fixture bytes, raw absent vs explicit version cases, future legacy version with retained known fields, foreign IDs, renamed/copied cross-profile files, direct typed/subrestore routes, preview enter/return failure, startup selection timing, three slots' backup independence and injected write failure. Synthetic negative cases may complement genuine fixtures but cannot replace missing history. No command to run a mutating harness is authorized by this report.

## 10. B-gate disposition and genuine risk register

Statuses describe **current evidence for the named safety proposition**, not management clearance or new implementation approval. “Proven” below means static/byte evidence within this report's limits, never compile/runtime verification.

| Gate | Disposition | Evidence and remaining boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **B-06 fixture provenance** | **UNPROVEN** for complete historical coverage | Genuine v8/v9 host archives, frozen v12 and documented v20/v21/v22 writer outputs exist (§8); no established complete v10–v24/missing-ID corpus. |
| **B-09 recovery** | **CONTRADICTED** as an existing safe recovery guarantee | Backup is notification only (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:286`); fallback overwrites main non-atomically (line 85); approved recovery design/runtime proof outstanding. |
| **B-10 pre-mutation admission** | **CONTRADICTED** for universal identity/lifecycle safety | Foreign scenario warning follows restoration (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1481`); startup already creates world; typed/subrestore/preview boundaries differ (§3). |
| **B-11 geometry extension safety** | **CONTRADICTED** for a drop-in third geometry | Current unknown models are refused, but size/generator silently select legacy for non-Enlarged80 if guards are extended alone (`Assets/ForestPrototype/StandGeometryModel.cs:34`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestStartingStand.cs:83`). |
| **B-13 new payload version contract** | **UNPROVEN** | Current plain DTO writes v24 (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:7`–9); no envelope/profile lineage or raw root-version contract exists; new rules require approval. |
| **B-14 genuine O2 identity** | **UNPROVEN** | Only ScenarioOne authored definition/ID found (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOne.asset:15`); ScenarioTwo species assets exist, but no authored O2 definition/scene/save identity found in tracked inventory. |
| **B-15 caller inventory** | **PROVEN** within static scope | §1 and Appendix A cover current literal entry references, including direct typed/subrestore and verification routes; dynamic/external invocation and runtime behavior remain unverified. |

Risks ordered by plausible historical-save loss/corruption exposure; ordering is qualitative engineering judgment, not measured incidence:

| Rank | Genuine risk | Source/evidence; mitigation requiring later approved work |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | New profile accidentally writes the historical single slot | Controller/menu hardcode same filename (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:15`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/StandaloneSessionMenu.cs:77`), no active-profile validation in Save (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:39`). Establish profile before capture/path/write and require negative same-slot witnesses. |
| 2 | Backup/overwrite failure loses valid bytes or makes recovery unavailable | Non-atomic fallback plus unconditional success-of-preservation message (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:84`–85, 49); backup rotates unvalidated old main (line 79); no backup loader. Qualify failure handling and preservation of original bytes. |
| 3 | Cross-world data admitted and later saved over historical slot | Foreign ID only warns after tree/ecology mutations (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1481`), empty ID accepted; public direct paths lack raw authentication (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:324`). Preserve actual legacy policy while adding approved strict new-profile authentication. |
| 4 | Future or ambiguous historical file silently reinterpreted/dropped on save | Warn+best effort load (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:346`), current-only capture DTO (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:7`–9), absent-version default differs from explicit <=0 normalization (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:327`). Do not tighten or round-trip unknown history without explicit authority/evidence. |
| 5 | Third geometry misinterprets cell/tree coordinates | `StandSizeMeters` else=40m and legacy generator fallback (`Assets/ForestPrototype/StandGeometryModel.cs:34`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestStartingStand.cs:83`); cell records depend on grid. Require explicit dispatcher and representative geometry/native checks. |
| 6 | Preview cannot restore own world, then permits saving preview | Return clears preview flag before restore and discards snapshot even on failure (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:725`–740); public Save unguarded (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveController.cs:39`). Preserve frozen bytes; qualify failed-return behavior and narrowly scoped restore authority. |
| 7 | Newly strict ID/version rules reject genuine historical saves | Partial provenance coverage (§8), initialized identity/version defaults (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestSaveData.cs:9`, 57). Establish genuine fixtures and policy before changing old-profile admissions. |
| 8 | Startup/direct subrestore partially changes state on refusal/exception | Awake world creation (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:447`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/ForestStartingStand.cs:52`), public subrestore first mutations (`Assets/ForestPrototype/ScenarioOne/ScenarioOneManager.cs:1456`), no transactional rollback in controller (line 349 onward). Requires lifecycle seam and exact no-mutation checks. |
| 9 | Tutorial/reset leaks between profiles | Shared prefix concatenation (`Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/LearningObjectivesView.cs:107`; `Assets/ForestPrototype/UI/MenuHelpView.cs:154`). Scope new preferences while retaining historical keys. Lower save-corruption risk, material player-progress risk. |

## Appendix C. Read-only artifact byte inventory

The metadata below was parsed from existing bytes without writing them. Sixteen LongHorizon490 files are reduced telemetry snapshots, **not ForestSaveData saves**: their Snapshot DTO has arm/year/trees/cohorts/cells rather than save version or ecologicalYear (`/media/jer/Files/CCF/Verification/scen1-ThreeSpeciesLongHorizon490-20261114T085531Z/harness/Scen1LongHorizon490Native.cs:63`, writer line 160). They are retained solely to make this false-positive exclusion reviewable; their absent version does **not** establish a genuine unversioned-save fixture. `ABSENT` means no raw field, not a deserializer result. `geometry ABSENT` on versions <20 dispatches as Legacy40 under `Assets/ForestPrototype/StandGeometryModel.cs:60`; explicit 1 means Enlarged80 under lines 15–16. File coordinates point to the raw version key (or line 1 for compressed archive/manifest). Provenance categories refer back to §8; otherwise origin is **NOT VERIFIED**. Duplicate copies are retained with their own paths and matching hashes, not counted as independent historical examples.
